## Remote log tail — ClickHouse death window (signoz_logs.distributed_logs_v2)

Death window per M22: [journal cut -> next boot], epoch-ns UTC. At a watchdog death the local journal loses its final ~30-90s to flush lag; when the OTel->ClickHouse pipeline is alive the remote tail is FRESHER than the journal cut (#36: newest row 5s before the cut), and when the collector died pre-death the pipeline is silently DARK (#37: 4h19m outage).
Window: 1795593467000000000 -> 1795593534000000000 epoch-ns UTC (2026-11-25 07:57:47 -> 2026-11-25 07:58:54 UTC)
PIPELINE DARK FOR THIS WINDOW — 0 rows in the window.
Per M22: ClickHouse absence is NOT evidence of absence — the collector has a fatal-at-startup failure mode that silences the whole log pipeline (proven #37). Cross-check signoz-collector liveness across this boot BEFORE trusting the gap.
```
```

## Annotation (2026-11-27): the "PIPELINE DARK" verdict above is FALSE for this boot

Measured with the script's own query plus the control window it lacks (`signoz_logs.distributed_logs_v2`, read-only, 2026-11-27 00:45 CET):

| Window | Rows | Newest row |
| ------ | ---- | ---------- |
| Control, the 600 s BEFORE the journal cut (epoch 1795593467) | 9958 | 1795593459354282000 ns, 7.6 s before the cut |
| Control, the last 60 s before the cut | 902 | same |
| Death window [cut -> next boot] | 0 | none |
| After the next boot started, first 120 s | 7065 | the pipeline emits again 0.14 s after the next boot begins |

The pipeline was ALIVE: its remote tail was 7.6 s behind the journal cut, so the death window of 60 s really holds 0 rows. The verdict has no positive control and reports "0 rows after the cut" as "pipeline dark". Correct verdict: `alive-behind` (the remote tail lags the cut; the journal is the better source for this window), not `dark`. The units explanation offered on 2026-11-26 00:36 ("second- or millisecond bounds return 0 rows") is wrong as well: the script's bounds are correct nanoseconds. Do not cite the DARK line as a finding. Source: `docs/status/2026-11-26_07-12_crash-followup-2-iopause-selection-rewritten-local-sim-green-vm-suite-red-at-6b-cap.md` section 0; control query of this boot: `~/.cache/freeze-triage-20261125/s3/ch-control-2boots.sh`.
